    % For linebreaks inside Verbatim environment from package fancyvrb.
    \makeatletter
        \newbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox
        \newbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox
        \newcommand*\Wrappedvisiblespace {\textcolor{red}{\textvisiblespace}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol {\textcolor{red}{\llap{\tiny$\m@th\hookrightarrow$}}}
        \newcommand*\Wrappedcontinuationindent {3ex }
        \newcommand*\Wrappedafterbreak {\kern\Wrappedcontinuationindent\copy\Wrappedcontinuationbox}
        % Take advantage of the already applied Pygments mark-up to insert
        % potential linebreaks for TeX processing.
        %        {, <, #, %, $, ' and ": go to next line.
        %        _, }, ^, &, >, - and ~: stay at end of broken line.
        % Use of \textquotesingle for straight quote.
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatspecials {%
            \def\PYGZus{\discretionary{\char`\_}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\_}}%
            \def\PYGZob{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\{}{\char`\{}}%
            \def\PYGZcb{\discretionary{\char`\}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\}}}%
            \def\PYGZca{\discretionary{\char`\^}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\^}}%
            \def\PYGZam{\discretionary{\char`\&}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\&}}%
            \def\PYGZlt{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\<}{\char`\<}}%
            \def\PYGZgt{\discretionary{\char`\>}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\>}}%
            \def\PYGZsh{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\#}{\char`\#}}%
            \def\PYGZpc{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\%}{\char`\%}}%
            \def\PYGZdl{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\$}{\char`\$}}%
            \def\PYGZhy{\discretionary{\char`\-}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\-}}%
            \def\PYGZsq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\textquotesingle}{\textquotesingle}}%
            \def\PYGZdq{\discretionary{}{\Wrappedafterbreak\char`\"}{\char`\"}}%
            \def\PYGZti{\discretionary{\char`\~}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\char`\~}}%
        }
        % Some characters . , ; ? ! / are not pygmentized.
        % This macro makes them "active" and they will insert potential linebreaks
        \newcommand*\Wrappedbreaksatpunct {%
            \lccode`\~`\.\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\.}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\.}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\,\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\,}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\,}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\;\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\;}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\;}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\:\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\:}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\:}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\?\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\?}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\?}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\!\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\!}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\!}}}%
            \lccode`\~`\/\lowercase{\def~}{\discretionary{\hbox{\char`\/}}{\Wrappedafterbreak}{\hbox{\char`\/}}}%
            \catcode`\.\active
            \catcode`\,\active
            \catcode`\;\active
            \catcode`\:\active
            \catcode`\?\active
            \catcode`\!\active
            \catcode`\/\active
            \lccode`\~`\~
        }
    \makeatother

    \let\OriginalVerbatim=\Verbatim
    \makeatletter
    \renewcommand{\Verbatim}[1][1]{%
        %\parskip\z@skip
        \sbox\Wrappedcontinuationbox {\Wrappedcontinuationsymbol}%
        \sbox\Wrappedvisiblespacebox {\FV@SetupFont\Wrappedvisiblespace}%
        \def\FancyVerbFormatLine ##1{\hsize\linewidth
            \vtop{\raggedright\hyphenpenalty\z@\exhyphenpenalty\z@
                \doublehyphendemerits\z@\finalhyphendemerits\z@
                \strut ##1\strut}%
        }%
        % If the linebreak is at a space, the latter will be displayed as visible
        % space at end of first line, and a continuation symbol starts next line.
        % Stretch/shrink are however usually zero for typewriter font.
        \def\FV@Space {%
            \nobreak\hskip\z@ plus\fontdimen3\font minus\fontdimen4\font
            \discretionary{\copy\Wrappedvisiblespacebox}{\Wrappedafterbreak}
            {\kern\fontdimen2\font}%
        }%

        % Allow breaks at special characters using \PYG... macros.
        \Wrappedbreaksatspecials
        % Breaks at punctuation characters . , ; ? ! and / need catcode=\active
        \OriginalVerbatim[#1,codes*=\Wrappedbreaksatpunct]%
    }
    \makeatother

\subsection{2879105W Weijia Wang}\label{w-weijia-wang}

    \section{1. Introduction}\label{introduction}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{1}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]

    \section{2. Integration and analysis of key
data}\label{integration-and-analysis-of-key-data}

\subsection{Data Import}\label{data-import}

The data set named ``Smart meters in London'' uploaded by Jean-Michel
(2018) in kaggle was used in this study. The data set mainly includes
two parts. The first part is energy consumption data for 5,567 London
households that participated in the UK Power Network-led Low Carbon
London project between November 2011 and February 2014, and the second
part is weather data for the London area during that period.

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{6}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{n+nd}{@memory}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cache}
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{read\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{file\PYZus{}path}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Get the paths of all CSV files in the folder}
    \PY{n}{csv\PYZus{}files} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{path}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{join}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{file\PYZus{}path}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{file}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{file} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{os}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{listdir}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{file\PYZus{}path}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{file}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{endswith}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{.csv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Read each CSV file as a DataFrame and store it in the list}
    \PY{n}{dataframes} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{file}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{file} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{csv\PYZus{}files}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Merge all DataFrames into one large DataFrame}
    \PY{n}{hhblock\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}dataframe} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{concat}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dataframes}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ignore\PYZus{}index}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{hhblock\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}dataframe}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{7}{\boxspacing}

\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{data\PYZus{}import}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{informations\PYZus{}households} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{archive/informations\PYZus{}households.csv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}dataset} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{archive/daily\PYZus{}dataset.csv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{halfhourly\PYZus{}block\PYZus{}0} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{archive/halfhourly\PYZus{}dataset/halfhourly\PYZus{}dataset/block\PYZus{}0.csv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{hhblock\PYZus{}0} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{archive/hhblock\PYZus{}dataset/hhblock\PYZus{}dataset/block\PYZus{}0.csv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{hhblock\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}dataframe} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{read\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{archive}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZbs{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hhblock\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZbs{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hhblock\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{uk\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{archive/uk\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays.csv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{weather\PYZus{}daily\PYZus{}darksky} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{read\PYZus{}csv}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{archive/weather\PYZus{}daily\PYZus{}darksky.csv}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{informations\PYZus{}households}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{halfhourly\PYZus{}block\PYZus{}0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hhblock\PYZus{}0}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{hhblock\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}dataframe}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{uk\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{weather\PYZus{}daily\PYZus{}darksky}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{weather\PYZus{}hourly\PYZus{}darksky}

    \subsection{informations\_households.csv}\label{informations_households.csv}

Informations\_house.csv contains information about each household, such
as household ID, household type, total electricity consumption of the
household, etc. Filter out lines with ACORN values of ``acorn-u'' and
``ACORN-'' for this dataset, which contain incomplete or unclear
records. Upon inspection, the processed data set does not contain
missing values.

    \subsection{daily\_dataset}\label{daily_dataset}

    When processing the daily\_dataset.csv dataset, convert the ``day''
column date string to type ``datetime64'' and use ``valid\_lclid'' to
filter out data with the same family ID. After that, a series of visual
charts show the characteristics of the key data in the dataset.

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{head}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1000}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{25}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{o}{@} \PY{n}{memory}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cache}
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{process\PYZus{}daily\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert \PYZsq{}day\PYZsq{} column to datetime format}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}datetime}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert \PYZsq{}LCLid\PYZsq{} column to datetime format}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{category}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Cleaning out data without Acorn information}
    \PY{n}{df} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{isin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{valid\PYZus{}lclid}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \subsubsection{Grouping Visualisation}\label{grouping-visualisation}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{31}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{plot\PYZus{}column\PYZus{}histogram}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{column\PYZus{}name}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

  consumption can be significant.

    \begin{center}
    \adjustimage{max size={0.9\linewidth}{0.9\paperheight}}{Descriptive visualisation_files/Descriptive visualisation_60_0.png}
    \end{center}
    { \hspace*{\fill} \\}
    
    \begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  From this scatter plot, it can be found that most of the data points
  are at the low end of the ``energy\_median'' range, which indicates
  that most of the time, the median energy use is low. While in the top
  half of the picture, there is a large cluster of points where
  ``energy\_median'' is low (near zero) and ``energy\_count'' is high
  (at the top of the Y-axis), this section indicates many days with
  lower median energy use but higher readings.
\end{itemize}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{49}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{plot\PYZus{}yearly\PYZus{}energy\PYZus{}trend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{stat\PYZus{}col}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{energy\PYZus{}mean}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{figure}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{figsize}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{14}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{8}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Get a list of years}
    \PY{n}{years} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{year}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{unique}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{year} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{sorted}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{years}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Select the current year\PYZsq{}s data}
        \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}year} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{year} \PY{o}{==} \PY{n}{year}\PY{p}{]}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Group by \PYZsq{}day\PYZsq{} and calculate the average electricity consumption for each day.}
        \PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}avg} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}year}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{stat\PYZus{}col}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Re\PYZhy{}indexing to ensure time\PYZhy{}series plotting is possible}
        \PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}avg} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}avg}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{reset\PYZus{}index}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Plotting average daily electricity consumption trends for the current year}
        \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{plot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}avg}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{daily\PYZus{}avg}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{stat\PYZus{}col}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{label}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n+nb}{str}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{year}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Setup Legend}
    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{legend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{title}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Year}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Setting up title and axis labels}
    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Yearly Trend of Daily Average }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{stat\PYZus{}col}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{capitalize}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{xlabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Day of Year}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{ylabel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Average }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{stat\PYZus{}col}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{capitalize}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Display Grid}
    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{grid}\PY{p}{(}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Display Graphics}
    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{show}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\PY{n}{plot\PYZus{}level}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{k}{lambda}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{plot\PYZus{}yearly\PYZus{}energy\PYZus{}trend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}daily\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \begin{center}

    \end{center}
    { \hspace*{\fill} \\}
    
    \begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  The chart shows the annual trend of average daily energy consumption
  from November 2011 to February 2014, from which it can be found that
  energy use has a clear seasonal fluctuation trend, with average energy
  consumption low in summer and high in winter.
\end{itemize}

    \subsection{hhblock\_dataset}\label{hhblock_dataset}

    For the hhblock\_dataset, the structure information for one of the
blocks was validated, and then all dataframes were merged into
hhBLOCK\_BIG\_dataframes, which were then converted to date and time,
checked for missing values, and visualized.

    \subsubsection{Data Viewing and
Cleaning}\label{data-viewing-and-cleaning}

\prompt{In}{incolor}{58}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{process\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}dataset}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert \PYZsq{}day\PYZsq{} column to datetime format}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}datetime}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert \PYZsq{}LCLid\PYZsq{} column to categorical data type}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{category}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Cleaning out data without Acorn information}
    \PY{n}{df} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{isin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{valid\PYZus{}lclid}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{62}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{o}{@} \PY{n}{memory}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cache}
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{data\PYZus{}for\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}plot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Select all numeric columns for group aggregation}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{iloc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{]}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Select all columns from the third to the last}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Means by year grouping}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}yearly\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Y}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Grouping by quarter to find the mean}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}quarterly\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Q}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Grouping by quarter to find the mean}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}monthly\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{M}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Get a string representation of the quarter and month, regardless of the year.}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} For example, convert \PYZdq{}2011Q4\PYZdq{} to \PYZdq{}Q4\PYZdq{} and 2012\PYZhy{}05 to \PYZdq{}M5\PYZdq{}.}
    \PY{n}{quarter\PYZus{}str} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}period}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Q}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{str}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{str}\PY{p}{[}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n}{month\PYZus{}str} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{month}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{apply}\PY{p}{(}\PY{k}{lambda} \PY{n}{x}\PY{p}{:} \PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{M}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{x}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Grouping by quarter to find the mean, here using the converted quarterly string}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}year\PYZus{}quarterly\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{quarter\PYZus{}str}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Group by month to find the mean, here use the converted month string}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}year\PYZus{}monthly\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{month\PYZus{}str}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Find the mean by grouping by day of the week}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}dayofweek\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dayofweek}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}yearly\PYZus{}mean}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}quarterly\PYZus{}mean}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}monthly\PYZus{}mean}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}year\PYZus{}quarterly\PYZus{}mean}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}year\PYZus{}monthly\PYZus{}mean}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}dayofweek\PYZus{}mean}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{center}
    \adjustimage{max size={0.9\linewidth}{0.9\paperheight}}{Descriptive visualisation_files/Descriptive visualisation_95_0.png}
    \end{center}
    { \hspace*{\fill} \\}
    
    \begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  It can be seen from many line plots that draw the average power
  consumption for every 30 minutes in a day that the second half of the
  night is the time when energy use is the least, while energy
  consumption shows an upward trend from 04:30 to 07:30-09:00 in the
  morning, and then starts to rise in the near evening until it reaches
  a peak in the evening, and then gradually declines, and so on.
\end{itemize}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{78}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{process\PYZus{}uk\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Bank holidays}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}datetime}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Bank holidays}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Bank holidays}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}datetime}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Bank holidays}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Create a collection with all the holiday dates}
    \PY{n}{holidays\PYZus{}set} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{set}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}bank\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Bank holidays}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Use .copy() to ensure that filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock is a separate copy to avoid subsequent modifications affecting the original data}
    \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}copy} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}hhblock}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{copy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}copy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{loc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{is\PYZus{}holiday}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}copy}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{isin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{holidays\PYZus{}set}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}copy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{iloc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{]}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Select the value column}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Finding the mean by grouping according to the is\PYZus{}holiday columns}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}holidays\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}copy}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{is\PYZus{}holiday}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Rename indexes to represent data more clearly}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}holidays\PYZus{}mean}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{out\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{in\PYZus{}holidays}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}holidays\PYZus{}mean}

    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{index}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{row} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{iterrows}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}

    \PY{n}{plt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{title}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Average Daily Electricity Consumption per 30 Minutes \PYZhy{} }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{title\PYZus{}suffix}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \begin{center}
    \adjustimage{max size={0.9\linewidth}{0.9\paperheight}}{Descriptive visualisation_files/Descriptive visualisation_103_0.png}
    \end{center}
    { \hspace*{\fill} \\}
    
    \begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  Comparing energy consumption on holidays with that on non-holidays, it
  can be found that the average start time of electricity consumption on
  holidays is slightly later than that on daily days, and daytime energy
  consumption is higher than daily electricity consumption, which may be
  caused by more people using electrical appliances at home during
  holidays (Kang and Reiner, 2022).
\end{itemize}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{data\PYZus{}for\PYZus{}acorn\PYZus{}plot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{merged\PYZus{}info\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Select all numeric columns for group aggregation}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{merged\PYZus{}info\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{iloc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{:}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{:}\PY{o}{\PYZhy{}}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{]}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Grouping by \PYZsq{}stdorToU\PYZsq{} to find means}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}std\PYZus{}tou\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{merged\PYZus{}info\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{stdorToU}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Finding the mean by \PYZsq{}Acorn\PYZsq{} grouping}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}acorn\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{merged\PYZus{}info\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Acorn}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Means by \PYZsq{}Acorn\PYZus{}grouped\PYZsq{} grouping}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}acorn\PYZus{}grouped\PYZus{}mean} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}numeric}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{groupby}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{merged\PYZus{}info\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Acorn\PYZus{}grouped}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{mean}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}std\PYZus{}tou\PYZus{}mean}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}acorn\PYZus{}mean}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}acorn\PYZus{}grouped\PYZus{}mean}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\end{itemize}

    \subsection{2.2 Weather Conditions}\label{weather-conditions}

    Energy consumption at the household level may vary from day to day due
to working hours, holidays, weather conditions, etc. (Kiprijanovska et
al., 2020), and different weather conditions may have different effects
on household energy consumption. The weather data used in this study was
collected by Jean-Michel (2018) using darksky api, including two
dimensions: daily weather data and hourly weather data. The daily data
mainly includes the maximum temperature of the day and its occurrence
time, the maximum perceived temperature of the day, the time when the
maximum perceived temperature occurred, wind speed, atmospheric
pressure, etc., while the hourly data mainly includes visibility, wind
direction, temperature, perceived temperature, and the specific time
point of data collection.

\end{tcolorbox}
        
    visibility -
能见度，通常以公里或英里为单位，表示在特定天气条件下最远可见的距离。
windBearing -
风向，表示风从哪个方向吹来。通常以角度表示，其中0度代表北风，90度代表东风，180度代表南风，270度代表西风。
temperature - 气温，通常以摄氏度或华氏度为单位，表示空气的热度。 time -
时间，数据采集的具体时间点，通常以UNIX时间戳或可读格式表示。 dewPoint -
露点温度，当空气冷却到无法容纳其中所有水蒸气时，水蒸气凝结成露水的温度。
pressure -
大气压强，表示空气的重量压在地面上的力量，通常以百帕斯卡（hPa）或毫巴（mb）为单位。
apparentTemperature -
体感温度，综合考虑风速、湿度和实际气温对人体感知温度的影响。 windSpeed -
风速，表示风的快慢，通常以每秒米数或每小时英里数表示。 precipType -
降水类型，如雨、雪、冰雹等。 icon -
天气图标的标识，用于直观表示天气状况，如晴天、多云、雨天等。 humidity -
湿度，表示空气中水蒸气的含量，通常以百分比表示。 summary -
天气概况的文字描述，提供对当前或预测天气状况的简短总结。

    { \hspace*{\fill} \\}
    
    \begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  This picture is one of the more interesting ones. According to the
  information conveyed by the picture, people feel the somatosensory
  temperature is lower when the ambient temperature is low, and the
  somatosensory temperature is higher when the ambient temperature is
  high.
\end{itemize}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{126}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{plot\PYZus{}weather\PYZus{}lineplot}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{column\PYZus{}name}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{time\PYZus{}type}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{k}{if} \PY{o+ow}{not} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{api}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{types}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{is\PYZus{}datetime64\PYZus{}any\PYZus{}dtype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}datetime}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Extract the corresponding time unit according to the time type}
    \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{time\PYZus{}type} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time\PYZus{}unit}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{hour}
    \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{time\PYZus{}type} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time\PYZus{}unit}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{date}
    \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{time\PYZus{}type} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{month}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time\PYZus{}unit}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{month}
    \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{time\PYZus{}type} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{quarter}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time\PYZus{}unit}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{quarter}
    \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{time\PYZus{}type} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{year}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time\PYZus{}unit}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{year}
    \PY{k}{else}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{raise} \PY{n+ne}{ValueError}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Invalid time\PYZus{}type provided. Choose from }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{day}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{month}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{quarter}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{year}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{.}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Plotting line graphs}

    \section{3. Time series clustering
analysis}\label{time-series-clustering-analysis}

To identify seasonal fluctuations in energy consumption, daily variation
patterns, and other key trends, this study chose to use time series
clustering to analyze energy use characteristics across different
households or time periods. Time series cluster analysis (Liao, 2005) is
a technique for grouping time series data, aiming to classify sequences
with similar time patterns into the same group. A series of steps such
as data preparation, feature extraction and cluster execution will be
carried out for time series cluster analysis.

    \subsection{3.1 Data Preparation}\label{data-preparation}

    \subsection{3.1.1 Timestamp alignment}\label{timestamp-alignment}

First, the household electricity consumption data is processed, which
needs to convert the time granularity. Since the household electricity
consumption data is measured in half an hour and the weather data is
measured in hours, the household electricity consumption data needs to
be converted into hourly units. This part is achieved by calculating the
average of two groups of half hour electricity consumption within each
hour.

    \subsubsection{3.1.2 Data Merging}\label{data-merging}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{(\arabic{enumi})}
\tightlist
\item
  Time format conversion Converts the day column of household
  electricity consumption data to the same date and hour format as the
  time column of weather data and merges the two data sets accordingly.
\end{enumerate}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{140}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{n+nd}{@memory}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cache}
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{turn\PYZus{}wide\PYZus{}to\PYZus{}long}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}hourly}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert LCLid columns to categorical data types}
    \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}hourly}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}hourly}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{category}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{n}{ddf} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{from\PYZus{}pandas}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}hourly}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{npartitions}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{32}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} First convert Dask DataFrame to Pandas DataFrame for melt operation}
    \PY{n}{pdf} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ddf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{compute}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Converting a Dask DataFrame back to a Pandas DataFrame}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Use Pandas\PYZsq{} melt function to perform the operation}
    \PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pdf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{melt}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{id\PYZus{}vars}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{LCLid}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{var\PYZus{}name}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{value\PYZus{}name}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{energy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert the \PYZsq{}hour\PYZsq{} column, extracting the hours from \PYZsq{}hh\PYZus{}X\PYZsq{} and converting them to integers}
    \PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{str}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extract}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZbs{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{d+)}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{int}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Merge \PYZsq{}day\PYZsq{} and \PYZsq{}hour\PYZsq{} into a datetime column}
    \PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}datetime}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{+} \PY{n}{pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}timedelta}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{unit}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{h}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Delete columns that are no longer needed}
    \PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{axis}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inplace}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} The processed Pandas DataFrame is then converted back to a Dask DataFrame}
    \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}melted} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{from\PYZus{}pandas}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{pdf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{npartitions}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{10}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Setting the progress bar}
    \PY{n}{pbar} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ProgressBar}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{pbar}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{register}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}result} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}melted}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{compute}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} cancellation progress bar}
    \PY{n}{pbar}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{unregister}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}result}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}parquet}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}hourly\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}row.parquet}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}result}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\PY{n+nd}{@memory}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cache}
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{combine\PYZus{}data\PYZus{}with\PYZus{}mapping}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}hourly\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}row}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{weather\PYZus{}hourly\PYZus{}darksky}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Initialise a dictionary to hold the transformation mapping}
    \PY{n}{mapping\PYZus{}dict} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Convert to dask dataframe}
    \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}weather} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{from\PYZus{}pandas}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{weather\PYZus{}hourly\PYZus{}darksky}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{npartitions}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{10}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Firstly the frequency of occurrence is calculated globally and a mapping dictionary is generated}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{col} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{precipType}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{icon}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{summary}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{col} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}weather}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{columns}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Use dask to calculate the number of occurrences of each value}
            \PY{n}{frequencies} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}weather}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{value\PYZus{}counts}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{compute}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Generate mapping based on frequency ordering}
            \PY{n}{mapping} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{value}\PY{p}{:} \PY{n}{i} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{value} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{enumerate}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{frequencies}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sort\PYZus{}values}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ascending}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{index}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{\PYZcb{}}
            \PY{n}{mapping\PYZus{}dict}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{mapping}

            \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Generate mapping based on frequency ordering}
        \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}weather}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}weather}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{col}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{map}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{mapping}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{meta}\PY{o}{=}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{x}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{int64}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{astype}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{int64}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
        
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Data consolidation using Dask}
    \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}result} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{from\PYZus{}pandas}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{filtered\PYZus{}big\PYZus{}hhblock\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}hourly\PYZus{}one\PYZus{}row}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{npartitions}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{10}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{combined\PYZus{}ddf} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{merge}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}result}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{ddf\PYZus{}weather}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{left\PYZus{}on}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{right\PYZus{}on}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{how}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{inner}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{pbar} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{ProgressBar}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{pbar}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{register}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{combined\PYZus{}pd} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{combined\PYZus{}ddf}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{compute}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{pbar}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{unregister}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{combined\PYZus{}pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{time}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{axis}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inplace}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{combined\PYZus{}pd}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}parquet}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{combined\PYZus{}hourly\PYZus{}weather.parquet}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Returns the merged DataFrame and mapping dictionary}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{combined\PYZus{}pd}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{mapping\PYZus{}dict}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \subsubsection{3.1.3 Data standardization}\label{data-standardization}

    \begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{(\arabic{enumi})}
\tightlist
\item
  Feature column generation Transform the overall time into a split time
  feature to better represent the periodicity of time.'' day-hour''
  -\textgreater{} ''hour'', ''day'', ''day\_of\_week'', ''month'',
  ''quarter'', ''year'' These periodic time features are then converted
  into cyclic features to better represent periodicity of time in the
  model.'' hour'' -\textgreater{} ''hour\_sin'', ''hour\_cos'' ''day''
  -\textgreater{} ''day\_sin'', ''day\_cos'' ''day\_of\_week''
  -\textgreater{} ''day\_of\_week\_sin'', ''day\_of\_week\_cos''
  ''month'' -\textgreater{} ''month\_sin'', ''month\_cos'' ''quarter''
  -\textgreater{} ''quarter\_sin'', ''quarter\_cos'' ''year''
  -\textgreater{} ''year\_sin'', ''year\_cos''
\end{enumerate}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{155}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{n+nd}{@memory}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cache}
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{add\PYZus{}cyclic\PYZus{}features}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Extracting periodic features from \PYZsq{}day\PYZhy{}hour\PYZsq{} columns}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{hour}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{day}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}of\PYZus{}week}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dayofweek}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{month}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{month}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{quarter}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{quarter}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{year}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{dt}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{year}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Converting hourly features to cyclic features}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour\PYZus{}sin}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{24}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour\PYZus{}cos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cos}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{24}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} \PYZsh{} Conversion days are cyclic features}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}sin}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{31}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}cos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cos}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{31}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Converting weekly features to cyclic features}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}of\PYZus{}week\PYZus{}sin}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}of\PYZus{}week}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{7}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}of\PYZus{}week\PYZus{}cos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cos}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}of\PYZus{}week}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{7}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Converting Monthly Features to Cyclic Features}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{month\PYZus{}sin}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{month}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{12}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{month\PYZus{}cos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cos}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{month}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{12}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Converting quarterly features to cyclical features}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{quarter\PYZus{}sin}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{quarter}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{quarter\PYZus{}cos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cos}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{quarter}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{4}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Converting year features to cyclic features}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{year\PYZus{}sin}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{sin}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{year}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{365}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{year\PYZus{}cos}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{cos}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{2} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{pi} \PY{o}{*} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{year}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{/} \PY{l+m+mi}{365}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Delete the original time signature column}
    \PY{n}{df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{drop}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZhy{}hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hour}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{day\PYZus{}of\PYZus{}week}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{month}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{quarter}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{year}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{axis}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inplace}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{True}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}parquet}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{cluster\PYZus{}cyclic\PYZus{}features.parquet}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

\prompt{In}{incolor}{160}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{compute\PYZus{}correlation}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{series\PYZus{}energy}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{column}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{series\PYZus{}column}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{series\PYZus{}energy\PYZus{}np} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{array}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{series\PYZus{}energy}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{series\PYZus{}column\PYZus{}np} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{array}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{series\PYZus{}column}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Calculating Pearson, Spearman and Kendall correlations}
    \PY{n}{pearson\PYZus{}corr} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{series\PYZus{}energy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{corr}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{series\PYZus{}column}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{method}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{pearson}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{spearman\PYZus{}corr} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{series\PYZus{}energy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{corr}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{series\PYZus{}column}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{method}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{spearman}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{kendall\PYZus{}corr} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{series\PYZus{}energy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{corr}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{series\PYZus{}column}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{method}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{kendall}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Calculate DTW distance}
    \PY{n}{dtw\PYZus{}distance} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{dtw}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{distance}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{series\PYZus{}energy\PYZus{}np}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{series\PYZus{}column\PYZus{}np}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{column}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{pearson\PYZus{}corr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{spearman\PYZus{}corr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{kendall\PYZus{}corr}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{dtw\PYZus{}distance}
\end{Verbatim}

    \begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
These columns are not in columns\_morethan0: \{'dewPoint', 'pressure', 'summary',
'windBearing', 'icon', 'temperature', 'apparentTemperature', 'LCLid', 'energy'\}
    \end{Verbatim}

    There is only one possible reason why sin and cos are highly correlated:
- Asymmetric effects: If the sensitivity of the target variable to time
is asymmetric, for example, if certain phenomena or behaviors are more
frequent or intense during certain periods of the day, this asymmetry
may cause one of the sin or cos to have a higher correlation with the
target variable. For example, if energy consumption decreases at night
but does not change much during the day, then the sin function (which
represents the change during the day) may have a higher correlation with
energy. - Intrinsic properties of the data: Some properties in the data
set may be more aligned with specific loop characteristics. For example,
if energy consumption mainly varies significantly over a particular
period of time, the pattern may be more reflected in changes in sin or
cos values.

According to the results, the features with low correlation are
excluded: ``dewPoint'', ``pressure'', ``icon'', ``summary''. At the same
time, although the correlation of ``temperature'' and
``apparentTemperature'' is not high, they are retained because they are
important features in meteorological data.

\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{std\PYZus{}scaler}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Initialising the StandardScaler}
    \PY{n}{scaler} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{StandardScaler}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Columns that need to be normalised.}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} sin, cos columns don\PYZsq{}t need to be normalised as they are already between 0 and 1.}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} precipType is categorical data and also doesn\PYZsq{}t need to be normalised}
    \PY{n}{norminal\PYZus{}columns} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{energy}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{visibility}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{windSpeed}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{humidity}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{temperature}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{apparentTemperature}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{]}
    
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}copy} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{df}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{copy}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Modify directly in place to save space}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} And this modifies the original DataFrame}
    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}copy}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{norminal\PYZus{}columns}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{scaler}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fit\PYZus{}transform}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df\PYZus{}copy}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{norminal\PYZus{}columns}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}copy}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{to\PYZus{}parquet}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{cluster\PYZus{}cyclic\PYZus{}features\PYZus{}normalised.parquet}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{df\PYZus{}copy}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \subsection{3.2 Time Series MiniBatchKMeans Clustering
execution}\label{time-series-minibatchkmeans-clustering-execution}

    \begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{(\arabic{enumi})}
\tightlist
\item
  Similarity measurement and clustering algorithm selection Dynamic Time
  Warping (DTW) DTW is a widely used time series similarity measurement
  method, which can flexibly compare sequences with different lengths or
  shifts in the timeline (Muller, 2007). By optimizing the alignment
  between sequences, DTW can quantify the similarity between them, even
  in the presence of time scaling, and DTW is suitable for processing
  data with time-varying properties. Mini-Batch K-Means Mini-Batch
  K-Means is a variant of the traditional K-Means algorithm that
  significantly reduces the computational cost of the algorithm by
  updating the cluster center with only one mini-batch of the data set
  in each iteration. This approach is particularly effective when
  dealing with large data sets because of its ability to reduce memory
  consumption and speed up clustering (Chavan, 2015). In the upcoming
  cluster analysis of this study, the data set expected to be used is a
  large-scale multimedia time series data set, and DTW is first
  considered as a similarity measure, because it can effectively deal
  with time migration and scaling between time series. Secondly,
  considering the computational efficiency of processing large data
  sets, Mini-Batch K-Means is chosen as the clustering method. A
  combination of Mini-Batch K-Means and DTW From the research object of
  this study, the combination of Mini-Batch K-Means and DTW is a
  strategy aimed at improving the clustering efficiency and effect of
  time series data. By combining the computational efficiency of
  Mini-Batch K-Means with the flexibility and accuracy of DTW in
  processing time series data, the efficiency of processing large data
  sets can be improved while retaining the advantages of DTW in time
  series data clustering.
\end{enumerate}

    \begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{(\arabic{enumi})}
\setcounter{enumi}{1}
\tightlist
\item
  Cluster execution
\end{enumerate}

    Clustering of single factors separately

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{189}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{compute\PYZus{}distance}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{j}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{distance}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{fastdtw}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{iloc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{]}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{iloc}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{j}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{distance}

\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{compute\PYZus{}distance\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{np}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{zeros}\PY{p}{(}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Compute the distance matrix in parallel and display the progress bar in conjunction with tqdm}
    \PY{n}{results} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{k}{with} \PY{n}{tqdm}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{desc}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{Processing fastdtw}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{total}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{o}{*}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{o}{+}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{o}{/}\PY{o}{/}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{as} \PY{n}{pbar}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{results}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{extend}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{Parallel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}jobs}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{32}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{delayed}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{compute\PYZus{}distance}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{j}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{)}
                                               \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{j} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
            \PY{n}{pbar}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{update}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples} \PY{o}{\PYZhy{}} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Filling the distance matrix}
    \PY{n}{idx} \PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{i} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{j} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}samples}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
            \PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{j}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{j}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{i}\PY{p}{]} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{results}\PY{p}{[}\PY{n}{idx}\PY{p}{]}
            \PY{n}{idx} \PY{o}{+}\PY{o}{=} \PY{l+m+mi}{1}

    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix}

\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{calculate\PYZus{}umap}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} umap = UMAP(n\PYZus{}components=2, random\PYZus{}state=42)}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} data\PYZus{}transformed = umap.fit\PYZus{}transform(distance\PYZus{}matrix)}
    \PY{n}{mds} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{MDS}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{dissimilarity}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{precomputed}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}components}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{2}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{random\PYZus{}state}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{42}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{normalized\PYZus{}stress}\PY{o}{=}\PY{k+kc}{False}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{mds}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fit\PYZus{}transform}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}

\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{fit\PYZus{}minibatchkmeans}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{mbkmeans} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{MiniBatchKMeans}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{batch\PYZus{}size}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{100}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{random\PYZus{}state}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{42}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}init}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{3}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{n}{labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{mbkmeans}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{fit\PYZus{}predict}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}\PY{p}{)}
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{mbkmeans}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{labels}

\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{cluster\PYZus{}features}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{5}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{mode}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hyper}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Calculate the DTW distance matrix}
    \PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{compute\PYZus{}distance\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} dimensionality}
    \PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{Parallel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}jobs}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{32}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{delayed}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{calculate\PYZus{}umap}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{distance\PYZus{}matrix}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}
    
    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} clustering}
    \PY{n}{results} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{Parallel}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}jobs}\PY{o}{=}\PY{l+m+mi}{32}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{delayed}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{fit\PYZus{}minibatchkmeans}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{\PYZus{}} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+m+mi}{1}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)}

    \PY{n}{mbkmeans}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{labels} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{results}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}
    
    \PY{k}{if} \PY{n}{mode} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{hyper}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Hyperparametric search mode}
        \PY{n}{inertia} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{mbkmeans}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{inertia\PYZus{}} \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Calculate the inertia value}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Calculate the contour coefficient}
        \PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avg} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}score}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{)} \PY{k}{if} \PY{n+nb}{len}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n+nb}{set}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{)} \PY{o}{\PYZgt{}} \PY{l+m+mi}{1} \PY{k}{else} \PY{l+m+mi}{0}

        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avg}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inertia}
    \PY{k}{elif} \PY{n}{mode} \PY{o}{==} \PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s1}{cluster}\PY{l+s+s1}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{p}{:}  \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} clustering pattern}
        \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{labels}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}\PY{p}{[}\PY{l+m+mi}{0}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{k}{else}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{k}{raise} \PY{n+ne}{ValueError}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Invalid mode. Choose from }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{hyper}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{ or }\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{cluster}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZsq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{.}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{194}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{k}{def} \PY{n+nf}{find\PYZus{}optimal\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{m}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
    \PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avgs} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}
    \PY{n}{inertia\PYZus{}values} \PY{o}{=} \PY{p}{[}\PY{p}{]}

    \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} Try different number of clusters}
    \PY{k}{for} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters} \PY{o+ow}{in} \PY{n+nb}{range}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{m}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n}\PY{p}{)}\PY{p}{:}
        \PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} print(\PYZsq{}Finding optimal clusters\PYZsq{})}
        \PY{n}{\PYZus{}}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avg}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{data\PYZus{}transformed}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inertia} \PY{o}{=} \PY{n}{cluster\PYZus{}features}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{df}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{features}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{o}{=}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avgs}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avg}\PY{p}{)}
        \PY{n}{inertia\PYZus{}values}\PY{o}{.}\PY{n}{append}\PY{p}{(}\PY{n}{inertia}\PY{p}{)}

        \PY{n+nb}{print}\PY{p}{(}\PY{l+s+sa}{f}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{Number of clusters: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{n\PYZus{}clusters}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, Silhouette Score: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avg}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{, Inertia: }\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZob{}}\PY{n}{inertia}\PY{l+s+si}{\PYZcb{}}\PY{l+s+s2}{\PYZdq{}}\PY{p}{)}
        
    \PY{k}{return} \PY{n}{silhouette\PYZus{}avgs}\PY{p}{,} \PY{n}{inertia\PYZus{}values}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{215}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} def extract\PYZus{}cluster\PYZus{}centers(df, labels):}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     \PYZsh{} Merge labels into raw data}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     df[\PYZsq{}cluster\PYZus{}label\PYZsq{}] = labels}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} }
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     \PYZsh{} Grouped according to cluster labels and calculated the mean for each grouping}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     cluster\PYZus{}centers = df.groupby(\PYZsq{}cluster\PYZus{}label\PYZsq{}).mean().iloc[:, 1:]}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} }
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     return cluster\PYZus{}centers}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

    \begin{tcolorbox}[breakable, size=fbox, boxrule=1pt, pad at break*=1mm,colback=cellbackground, colframe=cellborder]
\prompt{In}{incolor}{216}{\boxspacing}
\begin{Verbatim}[commandchars=\\\{\}]
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{} def visualize\PYZus{}cluster\PYZus{}centers(cluster\PYZus{}centers):}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     \PYZsh{} Visualisation of clustering centre eigenvalues}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     cluster\PYZus{}centers.plot(kind=\PYZsq{}bar\PYZsq{}, figsize=(12, 6))}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     plt.title(\PYZsq{}Cluster Centers\PYZsq{})}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     plt.xlabel(\PYZsq{}Cluster Label\PYZsq{})}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     plt.ylabel(\PYZsq{}Feature Value\PYZsq{})}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     plt.xticks(rotation=0)}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     plt.legend(loc=\PYZsq{}upper right\PYZsq{})}
\PY{c+c1}{\PYZsh{}     plt.show()}
\end{Verbatim}
\end{tcolorbox}

or less the same, and due to the length, only the above ideas are shown
here.
